TL;DR: 提出了一种“先竞争后协作”的多教师知识蒸馏框架，通过基于执行的裁判对四个前沿AI教师（Claude、Codex-GPT、Grok、Gemini）进行排名，然后协作构建可验证课程用于学生模型（Qwen2.5-Coder）训练。研究发现：饱和效应下教师解决标准问题近乎完美，但竞赛题存在明显差距；模仿（SFT）已验证解会降低已有能力的学生模型；而用协作课程作为RLVR环境能显著提升学生性能（+49%相对提升），表明教师协作的价值不在于聚合答案供模仿，而在于共同构建学生通过实践学习的环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM裁判的多教师蒸馏存在偏向自身输出的偏差，且缺乏对教师间能力差异的系统性比较与公平排名机制。

Method: 提出compete-then-collaborate框架：先用基于执行（单元测试与标准输入输出检查）的公平裁判对四个教师进行两两排名，再让教师协作构建可验证课程；对比了SFT（模仿）和RLVR（基于可验证奖励的强化学习）两种训练范式。

Result: (1) 饱和效应下标准题教师均近乎完美（99-100%），但竞赛题区分度明显（Gemini 77% > Claude 69% = Codex 69% > Grok 50%），然而教师排名与学生端结果无关；(2) SFT已验证解会降低已具备能力的学生（MBPP-test从76.7%降至72.7%，竞赛题从5.9%降至2.9%）；(3) 协作课程作为RLVR环境提升学生竞赛题表现（5.9%至8.8%，+49%相对增益），逆转SFT方向。

Conclusion: 多教师协作的价值不在于聚合答案供模仿，而在于共同构建一个可验证的环境让学生通过实践学习；RLVR相比SFT更适合利用教师协作课程。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as teachers generating training data for smaller students. Prior multi-teacher knowledge distillation methods merge outputs without determining which frontier model teaches best, often relying on an LLM judge biased toward its own outputs. We introduce a compete-then-collaborate framework where four frontier AI teachers (Claude, Codex-GPT, Grok, Gemini) are ranked head-to-head by an execution-based judge (unit tests and stdin-stdout checks) with fairness controls, and then collaborate to build a verifiable curriculum for a student (Qwen2.5-Coder). We report three findings. (1) Under execution verification, all teachers solve standard problems near-perfectly after self-correction (99-100%) due to a saturation effect, but harder competition problems separate them (Gemini 77% > Claude 69% = Codex 69% > Grok 50%); however, the robust student-side results do not depend on teacher ranking. (2) Imitation (SFT) on verified solutions does not improve, and can degrade, an already-competent student at 7B and 32B (e.g., from 76.7% to 72.7% on MBPP-test, and 5.9% to 2.9% on competition problems). (3) Using the same collaborative curriculum as a reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) environment improves the student (from 5.9% to 8.8% peak on competition problems, a +49% relative gain), reversing SFT's direction. The value of AI-teacher collaboration lies not in pooling answers to imitate, but in jointly constructing a verifiable environment where the student learns by doing. We release a reproducible on-prem pipeline (NVIDIA GB10) with framework patches for running GRPO on a bleeding-edge stack.

</details>


### [147] [MentalHospital: A Virtual Environment for Evaluating Psychiatric Clinical Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08257)
*Yuming Yang,Xiao Sun,Yuanwei Zou,Zhengxiao Wu,Yun Chen,Jiang Zhong,Haoyang Zeng,Jingwang Huang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MentalHospital——一个基于LLM的精神科临床接诊虚拟评估环境，通过S.O.A.P.工作流、1193例精神科电子病历构建的技能增强标准化病人，以及双轨评估协议，系统评估LLM在完整精神科诊疗中的能力。同时开发MentalEval五个领域评估器，实验表明最强LLM在客观精神科能力上仍落后临床医生37.28个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在孤立精神科任务（对话、诊断、治疗规划）上表现良好，但缺乏模拟完整精神科临床接诊的基准评估环境。

Method: 1) MentalHospital环境：基于S.O.A.P.工作流，使用1193例脱敏精神科电子病历构建技能增强标准化病人，覆盖ICD-11主要类别和76种障碍。2) 双轨评估协议：客观对照EHR参考标准+主观临床过程质量评估。3) MentalEval评估器：五个领域专用评估器（共情沟通、访谈专业度、临床笔记质量、诊断严谨性、治疗适当性），采用基于评分标准的SFT和专家引导的DPO训练。

Result: 1) 22名临床医生调查支持MentalHospital的临床保真度（3.88/5）。2) MentalEval实现强专家一致性，平均QWK为0.944。3) 最强LLM在客观精神科能力上落后临床医生37.28个百分点，精神状态评估是关键瓶颈。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在完整精神科临床接诊场景下与临床医生存在显著差距，精神状态评估是最薄弱的环节，MentalHospital和MentalEval为评估和改进LLM精神科临床能力提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on isolated psychiatric tasks, including dialogue, diagnosis, and treatment planning, yet existing benchmarks rarely simulate complete psychiatric clinical encounters. We introduce $\textbf{MentalHospital}$, a virtual evaluation environment for LLM-based psychiatric clinical encounters. MentalHospital instantiates the Subjective Interviewing, Objective Examination, Diagnostic Assessment, and Treatment Planning (S.O.A.P.) workflow, using skill-augmented standardized patients constructed from 1,193 de-identified psychiatric electronic health record (EHR) cases spanning all major ICD-11 categories and 76 disorders. Each encounter is assessed through a dual-track protocol that combines objective comparison against EHR-derived references with subjective assessment of clinical process quality. To scale specialist judgment, we develop $\textbf{MentalEval}$, five domain-specific evaluators covering communication empathy, interviewing professionalism, clinical-note quality, diagnostic rigor, and treatment appropriateness, trained with rubric-grounded SFT and expert-guided DPO. Survey responses from 22 clinicians support MentalHospital's clinical fidelity (3.88/5), while MentalEval achieves strong expert alignment with an average QWK of 0.944. Benchmarking shows that even the strongest LLM trails clinicians by 37.28 percentage points in objective psychiatric competence, with mental status assessment as a key bottleneck.

</details>


### [148] [Different Teachers, Different Capabilities: Sub-1B On-Device Distillation for Structured Text Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08268)
*Vinay Kumar Chaganti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究将大模型（8B）的结构化提取任务蒸馏到小模型（0.6B）的效果。蒸馏后的学生模型速度提升约48倍（0.8s vs 39s），在摘要质量上恢复了58%的师生差距，但不同教师蒸馏出的能力存在差异：推理型教师提升写作质量，管理型管道提升标签多样性。未能在所有指标上胜出，最终产出是按字段的路由图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模结构化提取任务中，每项都调用大模型会导致高延迟和高成本，因此将任务蒸馏到小模型（设备端模型）是一种有吸引力的方案，可以在大幅降低时间和成本的同时获得可比的输出。

Method: 将8B的推理教师（deepseek-r1:8b）通过QLoRA蒸馏到0.6B的学生模型（Qwen3-0.6B），使用三个随机种子。设置两个教师对照组：同规模非推理教师和更大规模的管理型管道。采用盲法、无参考、三评委小组对所有方案进行评分，并与两个非蒸馏基线（少样本提示和约束解码）比较。

Result: 学生模型每篇文章约0.8秒，教师约39秒；学生恢复了58%的基础到教师的摘要质量差距，比约束解码基线高16.8分，比少样本提示高4.9分。同规模非推理教师训练的学生不优于未微调的基础模型。推理教师转移了写作质量，管理型管道转移了标签多样性，但推理教师的学生在短篇、薄源文章上编造内容（74 vs 55忠实）。

Conclusion: 没有单一引擎在所有领域获胜，最终的交付成果是按字段的路径路由图，用于设备端内容增强。

Abstract: High-volume structured extraction pays a large model's latency on every item, so distilling the task into a small on-device model is attractive: comparable output at a fraction of the time and cost. We measure what that distillation actually delivers, per sub-task. Each news article is mapped to one JSON object with a short summary and five categorical labels. We distill an 8B reasoning teacher (deepseek-r1:8b) into a 0.6B student (Qwen3-0.6B; QLoRA, three seeds), and add two teacher controls: a same-size non-reasoning teacher and a larger managed pipeline. A blinded, reference-free, three-judge panel scores every arm against the full article, alongside two non-distillation baselines, few-shot prompting and constrained decoding. The student runs at about 0.8 s per article against the teacher's 39 s, and recovers 58% of the base-to-teacher gap on summary quality, beating its primary baseline (constrained decoding) by +16.8 points and few-shot prompting by a secondary +4.9. A same-size non-reasoning teacher trains a student no better than the untuned base, so the summary gain follows from the teacher's reasoning nature rather than its scale. Capabilities then split by teacher: the reasoning teacher transfers writing quality and the managed pipeline transfers label diversity, while a same-size instruction teacher's students stay more grounded on the 22 short, thin-source articles in the 93-item test set (74 versus 55 faithful), where the reasoning-lineage student fabricates. That grounding difference is a consistent ordering rather than a significant aggregate effect, and the subgroup is small, so we report it as a direction. Because no single engine wins every field, the deliverable is a per-field routing map for on-device enrichment.

</details>


### [149] [INTENT: An LSTM Framework for Vehicle Intention Prediction in Intersection Scenarios with Comprehensive Ablation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08316)
*Logine M. Zaki,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出INTENT框架，利用LSTM模型在交叉路口提前2秒预测车辆意图（直行、左转、右转），在InD数据集上达到99.71%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升自动驾驶车辆在复杂驾驶场景（如交叉路口、环岛、紧急情况）中的安全性和敏捷性，通过预测驾驶员意图来辅助采取正确的避让行动，避免灾难发生。

Method: 提出INTENT框架，采用LSTM模型，在事件发生前2秒预测车辆在交叉路口的意图（直行、左转、右转），并在InD数据集上进行多种模型实验和消融研究。

Result: 在InD数据集上达到99.71%的准确率。

Conclusion: INTENT框架能够有效提前预测车辆意图，且意图预测可用于增强轨迹预测（意图条件轨迹预测），提升自动驾驶的安全性。

Abstract: Vehicle intention prediction is a pivotal aspect in the agility and safety of autonomous vehicles in all driving scenarios; if genuine enhancement of autonomous vehicles are required, we need to make them adopt human interpretation of driver's intention especially in cases that require a lot of human interaction as well as complex driving behaviors like the ones at intersections, roundabouts and emergency cases such as sudden stops where vehicle intention prediction helps in taking the correct evasive action within a real time period where every second of action makes an impact and can prevent a catastrophe from taking place. In the worst case, it helps minimize the damage and make safety a priority. Intention prediction can also be used to enhance trajectory prediction (intention conditioned trajectory prediction). In this study, The INTENT framework is proposed using LSTM model to predict the vehicle's intention at intersections 2 seconds ahead of the event occurrence to predict whether the cars in intersections are going straight, turning left, or turning right. Various model experiments and ablation study are thoroughly tested on InD dataset achieving 99.71% accuracy.

</details>


### [150] [PolyUQuest: Verifiable Structure-Aware Web RAG over Heterogeneous Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08269)
*Ying Liu,Yi Ye,Quanyu Feng,Mingxi Ye,Mingtao Zhang,Haoyang Li,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PolyUQuest 是一种基于异构图的、结构感知的 Web RAG 框架，通过统一页面间的超链接拓扑、页面内的 DOM 层次结构以及跨页面的实体-关系知识来增强检索增强生成，并使用两级路由器将查询分配到三种检索模式之一，且所有答案均可完全追溯验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 系统将网页视为纯文本，丢失了 HTML 中编码的结构和语义信号，导致检索质量受限。

Method: 构建异构图表征超链接拓扑、DOM 层次结构和实体关系；设计两级路由器按结构需求将查询分发至直接块检索、跨页面图遍历和多跳实体推理三种模式；每个被引用的块携带来源页面、标题路径和实体链接以实现完全可验证性。

Result: 在包含 4,240 个页面、31,086 个 DOM 块、29,119 个实体和 37,680 个关系的 PolyU 官方网站数据集上，PolyUQuest 在答案正确性、覆盖率和忠实度方面优于现有 RAG 系统，且每个查询消耗的 LLM token 显著更少。

Conclusion: PolyUQuest 展示了结构感知的 Web RAG 在学术网站场景中的有效性，并正在部署为面向学生的问答服务。

Abstract: Existing retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems treat web pages as flat text, losing the structural and semantic signals encoded in HTML. We present PolyUQuest, a verifiable, structure-aware web RAG framework built on a heterogeneous graph that unifies hyperlink topology between pages, DOM hierarchy within pages, and entity-relation knowledge across pages. A two-tier router dispatches each query to one of three retrieval modes matched to its structural need, including direct block retrieval, cross-page graph traversal, and multi-hop entity reasoning. Every answer is fully verifiable, as each cited block carries its source page, heading path, and entity links so that users can trace any claim back to its structural evidence. We evaluate on the official websites of the Hong Kong Polytechnic University (PolyU), comprising 4,240 pages, 31,086 DOM blocks, 29,119 entities, and 37,680 relations, together with a multi-type evaluation benchmark. PolyUQuest outperforms existing RAG systems in answer correctness, coverage, and faithfulness, while consuming significantly fewer LLM tokens per query. The demonstration provides an interactive interface for inspecting cited answers, comparing retrieval traces across routing modes, and exploring evidence graph paths. PolyUQuest is being prepared for deployment as a student-facing QA service at PolyU.

</details>


### [151] [Understanding Axes of Difficulty For Long Context Tasks Via PredicateLongBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08284)
*Siddhartha Jain,Ameya Velingker*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PredicateLongBench是一个通过寻找满足特定约束的最长连续子序列来压力测试LLM长上下文推理能力的基准，它沿着多个难度轴系统评估模型表现，发现当前最先进模型在任务难度提升时表现挣扎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文评估主要测量平均性能，许多已经饱和或缺乏鲁棒性，缺少系统性地探究模型在不同难度轴上的表现的方法。

Method: 提出PredicateLongBench基准，要求模型从长输入中识别满足给定谓词/约束的最长连续单词子序列；提供全合成和基于真实文档的两种生成管线；沿多个不同的难度轴系统探索模型能力。

Result: 前沿模型在任务难度沿各轴提升时表现挣扎，表明当前长上下文能力存在局限性。

Conclusion: PredicateLongBench能够有效揭示当前长上下文模型的局限性，且任务概念简单、无需基于LLM的生成或评判。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated rapidly improving long-context capabilities, prompting a wave of benchmarks designed to evaluate them. However, existing long-context evaluations - from Needle-in-a-Haystack (NIAH) tests to more recent multi-hop reasoning and summarization tasks - predominantly measure average-case performance, and many are either saturated or lack robustness. Notably absent is a systematic way to probe how models perform as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along various axes. We address this gap by proposing PredicateLongBench, a benchmark that stress-tests long-context reasoning by asking models to identify the longest contiguous subsequence of words in a long input that satisfies given predicates/constraints (e.g., lexicographic ordering), drawn from a broader predicate class. The central innovation of our benchmark is the identification and systematic exploration of multiple different axes of difficulty which test multiple aspects of long context understanding. We provide two complementary generation pipelines - a fully synthetic setup using random word-like strings, and a real-world setup that samples words from natural documents while preserving their distributional properties. We find that frontier models struggle to perform well as we scale up the difficulty of tasks along our axes, demonstrating the utility of our benchmark in understanding the limitations of current long-context capabilities. Furthermore, the tasks in PredicateLongBench, though challenging, are conceptually simple and do not require LLM-based generations or judges.

</details>


### [152] [Psychological Competence as a Missing Dimension in AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08285)
*Marcos Economides,Paul M. Sacher,Samuel Salzer,Alexis Michelle Abellar,Fendi Tsim,Antoine Ferrère*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"心理胜任力"作为人机交互型AI系统评估中缺失的关键维度，定义了其核心概念、边界及评估方法，主张将其纳入AI评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估框架过度聚焦技术性能（如准确率、鲁棒性、推理能力），忽视了与用户直接交互的AI系统（如顾问、教练、导师、伴侣）在认知、情感、信念、信任和决策方面对用户产生的心理影响，评估单元应从模型扩展至人机交互本身。

Method: 基于行为科学和人机交互研究，提出心理胜任力的概念框架，明确其核心域（包括框架效应、语气、感知权威、响应性、不确定性处理、对话引导等交互属性），并描述三种评估方法：基于场景的探测、结构化人工评估、模型辅助评估。

Result: 定义了心理胜任力的概念与边界，指出现有评估方法虽部分涉及该问题但鲜有直接评估心理效应，提出了可操作的评估方法路径。

Conclusion: 心理胜任力应成为模型提供方、部署组织、研究者和监管机构在人机交互型AI系统评估中的核心考量。

Abstract: Current AI evaluation frameworks focus primarily on technical performance, including accuracy, robustness, reasoning ability, and policy compliance. These measures remain essential, but they are not sufficient for systems that interact directly with users through natural language. Human-facing AI systems are increasingly used as advisors, coaches, tutors, and companions. In these roles, their responses can shape how users reason, interpret emotions, form beliefs, calibrate trust, and make decisions. The relevant unit of evaluation is therefore not only the model, but the human-AI interaction. This paper introduces psychological competence as a missing dimension in AI evaluation. We define psychological competence as the capacity of a human-facing AI system to support user cognition, emotional interpretation, and behavioral decision-making in ways that are appropriate to the user, context, and purpose of the interaction. This includes interaction properties such as framing, tone, perceived authority, responsiveness, uncertainty handling, and conversational guidance. Existing evaluation approaches capture parts of this problem but rarely assess these psychological effects directly. Drawing on behavioral science and human-AI interaction research, we outline a conceptual framework for psychological competence and its core domains. Rather than proposing a specific benchmark, we define the construct, clarify its boundaries, and describe how it may be assessed through scenario-based probes, structured human evaluation, and model-assisted evaluation methods. We argue that psychological competence should become a core consideration for model providers, deploying organizations, researchers, and regulators concerned with the real-world effects of human-facing AI systems.

</details>


### [153] [Blind-Spots-Bench: Evaluating Blind Spots in Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08317)
*Matteo Santelmo,Xiuying Wei,Israa Fakih,Felix Bauer,Juan Garcia Giraldo,Chengkun Li,Etienne Bamas,Emmanuel Abbé*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 blind-spots-bench，一个由 235 个对人类简单但对现代 AI 困难的任务组成的基准，用于暴露当前模型的盲点。评估发现闭源前沿模型在整体上优于开源模型约 10%，但没有任何模型在所有任务类型上占优，部分任务对所有模型均具挑战性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试中表现良好的 AI 模型在人类视为简单的任务（如操作字符串、画五条腿的狗）上仍然失败，表明当前基准可能低估了模型的系统性盲点，亟需一个能暴露这些缺陷的诊断性测试。

Method: 从 AI 课程学生中收集原始问题，经清洗和结构化参考标注后构建包含 235 个样本的数据集，并提出相应的任务分类法；开发自动化评分管线，评估包括开源和闭源的语言、视觉-语言及图像生成模型。

Result: 闭源前沿模型比开源模型整体高出约 10% 的准确率，尽管它们在现有基准上表现相当；细粒度分析显示没有单一模型在所有任务类型上占优，且部分任务对所有模型都具挑战性。

Conclusion: blind-spots-bench 作为一个诊断性压力测试，能有效识别当前模型的具体弱点，对推动 AI 系统在简单但易出错的任务上的改进具有重要价值。

Abstract: Modern AI models achieve strong performance on many established benchmarks, yet they still fail on tasks that humans find almost trivial, such as manipulating a string or drawing a dog with five legs. These examples suggest that existing benchmarks may under-measure persistent blind spots in current systems. We introduce $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$, a benchmark designed to expose such blind spots through tasks that appear simple for humans but remain challenging for modern AI. We collect raw questions from students in an AI course, clean and annotate them with structured reference solutions, and propose a task taxonomy tailored to the resulting dataset of 235 samples. We further develop an automated grading pipeline to evaluate a wide range of models, including open-weight and closed-source language, vision-language, and image-generation models. Our analysis on $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ reveals that closed-source frontier models can substantially outperform open-weight models with even $\approx10\%$ gap, even when they attain comparable performance on existing benchmarks. A more fine-grained analysis shows that no single model dominates across all task types, and that some tasks remain challenging for all evaluated models. These results highlight the value of $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ as a diagnostic stress test for identifying concrete weaknesses in current modern models.

</details>


### [154] [MobiDiff: Semantic-Aware Multi-Channel Discrete Diffusion for Human Mobility Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08357)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Taichi Liu,Desheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MobiDiff，一个端到端离散扩散框架，通过直接对多通道语义骨架去噪来高效生成人类移动数据，避免了现有方法中昂贵的插值和粗到细的管线。在三个真实数据集上，MobiDiff在保真度、隐私保护和效率方面均表现优异，推理速度比GeoGen平均快5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实人类移动数据收集成本高且因隐私问题难以共享；现有扩散方法依赖连续或潜在时空轨迹，无法原生建模具有显式区域、活动、时间和间隔结构的离散语义事件。

Method: MobiDiff将每个签到事件分解为空间、活动和时间三个通道，采用结构化的事件级、组级和通道级掩码机制，以端到端方式直接对多通道语义骨架进行离散去噪。

Result: 在亚特兰大、波士顿和西雅图三个大规模真实数据集上，MobiDiff有效保持了轨迹长度和时间间隔分布，在更广泛的移动统计数据上具有竞争力，且推理速度比GeoGen平均快5.3倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为合成移动数据生成提供了一种可解释且高效的框架。

Abstract: Human mobility data are essential for transportation optimization, urban planning, and resource allocation, yet real-world mobility data are costly to collect and difficult to share due to privacy concerns. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown promise in synthesizing realistic mobility patterns, but they typically rely on continuous or latent spatio-temporal traces, limiting their ability to natively model discrete semantic events with explicit region, activity, time, and interval structures. To address this issue, we introduce MobiDiff, an end-to-end discrete diffusion framework that efficiently generates mobility data by directly denoising multi-channel semantic skeletons, avoiding the costly interpolation, latent trace construction, and coarse-to-fine realization pipelines widely used in existing diffusion-based methods. Specifically, MobiDiff decomposes each human check-in event into spatial, activity, and temporal channels, and employs structured event-, group-, and channel-level masking to jointly capture trajectory-level mobility patterns and within-event dependencies. We evaluate generation fidelity, privacy-preserving, and efficiency on three large-scale real-world datasets from Atlanta, Boston, and Seattle. Results show that MobiDiff effectively preserves trajectory length and temporal interval distributions while remaining competitive across broader mobility statistics; it is also much faster than state-of-the-art methods, e.g., 5.3$\times$ faster than GeoGen on average during inference. These findings suggest that discrete diffusion offers an interpretable and efficient framework for synthetic mobility data generation.

</details>


### [155] [FedOPAL: One-Shot Federated Learning via Analytic Visual Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08368)
*Lingyu Qiu,Daniela Annunziata,Stefano Izzo,Fabio Giampaolo,Francesco Piccialli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出FedOPAL框架，通过在客户端引入视觉提示作为特征校正器，结合局部近端约束将非独立同分布数据的特征分布调整至线性可分空间，从而满足解析联邦学习的理论假设。实验表明，该方法在保持零服务器端训练成本的同时，性能显著优于原始解析方法，并达到与迭代式方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在边缘智能中广泛部署后，通信带宽成为联邦学习可扩展性的核心瓶颈。一次性联邦学习虽减少了通信轮次，但现有迭代微调或知识蒸馏方法仍面临服务器端计算成本高和超参数敏感问题。解析联邦学习虽实现了高效的无梯度聚合，但在非独立同分布数据环境下静态特征假设失效，导致特征流形错位，严重损害模型性能。

Method: 提出FedOPAL框架，将视觉提示作为特征校正器，通过施加局部近端约束，主动将异构数据的特征分布校正至线性可分空间，从而满足解析联邦学习的理论假设，实现高效的梯度无关聚合。

Result: FedOPAL在多个基准测试上显著优于原始解析方法，同时保持零服务器端训练成本，准确率达到与最先进的迭代式方法相当的水平。

Conclusion: FedOPAL为边缘端大模型的高效协作提供了新的工程范式，在不增加服务器计算负担的前提下解决了非独立同分布数据下的特征流形错位问题。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of basic models in edge intelligence, communication bandwidth has become a core bottleneck restricting the scalability of federated learning. Although one-shot federated learning alleviates this problem by minimizing communication rounds, existing iterative fine-tuning or knowledge distillation methods still face challenges such as high server-side computational costs and hyperparameter sensitivity. Analytical federated learning achieves efficient gradientfree aggregation using least-squares closed-form solutions, but in environments with non-independent and identically distributed data, its static feature assumptions fail, leading to feature manifold misalignment and severely impairing model performance. To address this contradiction, this paper proposes the FedOPAL framework. This framework adapts the visual prompts as feature rectifiers, actively correcting the feature distribution of heterogeneous data to a linearly separable space by applying local proximal constraints, thereby satisfying the theoretical assumptions of analytical federated learning. Experimental results show that FedOPAL not only significantly outperforms the original analytical methods on several benchmarks, but also achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art iterative methods while maintaining zero server-side training costs, providing a new engineering paradigm for efficient collaboration of large models on the edge.

</details>


### [156] [Towards Mechanistically Understanding Why Memorized Knowledge Fails to Generalize in Large Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08393)
*Lu Dai,Ziyang Rao,Yili Wang,Hanqing Wang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大语言模型在微调注入新知识时存在"知道-使用鸿沟"：模型能快速记忆新事实但无法用于下游推理。通过自修补技术发现这是由于知识电路错配——记忆表征存在但未被路由到有效计算层，简单启发式策略可恢复58-75%的泛化失败空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型微调注入新知识时，模型能快速记忆新事实却无法将其用于下游推理任务，这一现象缺乏系统性理解和形式化定义。

Method: 1）形式化定义"知道-使用鸿沟"（准确率差距+时间滞后）；2）提出自修补（self-patching）干预技术，通过定位激活位置并重表征来改善泛化失败案例；3）验证知识电路错配假设：记忆表征存在但未被路由到计算有效层。

Result: 自修补识别出表征位置错配是泛化失败的原因；基于此发现的简单启发式策略可恢复58-75%的oracle空间；跨领域实验验证鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 微调新知识时，记忆与使用之间的鸿沟源于知识电路错配（表征存在但路由错误），通过干预表征位置可显著缓解，为改进知识注入提供诊断工具和实用策略。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs to inject new knowledge faces a critical challenge: LLMs can quickly memorize new facts, yet fail to use them for downstream reasoning tasks. We formalize this failure as the \textit{\textbf{Knowing--Using Gap}}, characterized by an accuracy gap and a temporal lag between memorization and generalization. To understand this phenomenon, we fine-tune LLMs with unseen knowledge and monitor the spatial permeation dynamics of the knowledge internally using a novel intervention technique called self-patching. Self-patching identifies activation locations where relocating representations substantially improves failed generalization cases. These results are consistent with a knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis: memorized representations can exist internally but may not be routed to computation-effective layers. To demonstrate the practicality of this diagnostic finding, we design a simple heuristic strategy which recovers 58--75\% of the oracle headroom in generalization failure. Experiments are done cross-domain for the robustness of this finding.

</details>


### [157] [Game Theory Driven Multi-Agent Framework Mitigates Language Model Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08403)
*Runzhe Liu,Biquan Bie,Zihao Wang,Yuchao Ma,Yexin Liu,Xinghai Li,Harry Yang,Wenbo Yang,Jinzhe Cao,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Frame，一个结合贝叶斯和团队博弈原理的自适应多智能体框架，通过自动化闭环数据合成和模型训练，迫使大语言模型内化领域约束进行结构化推理，最终训练出7B参数模型OmniChem，在化学基准测试中达到GPT-4o mini水平，幻觉率较基础架构降低79.46%，并在分子设计和合成规划中展现高级能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级大语言模型在基于规则的科学领域中严重受限，它们倾向于模仿语言模式而非复现公理推理，导致频繁产生幻觉。现有模型缺乏对领域约束的内化能力，无法可靠地执行科学推理任务。

Method: G-Frame框架融合贝叶斯原理和团队博弈理论，构建自适应多智能体系统，实现高质量数据合成与模型训练的自动化闭环。通过结构化推理强制模型内化领域约束，合成了363,045条思维链和199,589个问答对，最终训练出7B参数的OmniChem模型。

Result: OmniChem在自定义基准测试和ChemBench上与GPT-4o mini性能持平；相对于基础架构，幻觉率降低79.46%；在分子设计和合成规划等高级任务中展现出先进能力。

Conclusion: 本文建立了利用自适应多智能体克服固有推理缺陷的可扩展范式，为加速科学专业知识发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The application of lightweight Large Language Models in rule-based scientific domains remains severely limited by their tendency to mimic linguistic patterns rather than reproduce axiomatic reasoning, causing frequent hallucinations. Here, we show that G-Frame, an adaptive multi-agent framework integrating Bayesian and team game principles, establishes an automated closed-loop for high-quality data synthesis and model training. By forcing the internalization of domain constraints through structured reasoning, we synthesized a specialized corpus of 363,045 chains-of-thought and 199,589 question-answer pairs. The resulting 7B model OmniChem achieves performance parity with GPT 4o mini on custom benchmarks and ChemBench while exhibiting a 79.46% reduction in hallucinations relative to its base architecture. We further demonstrate the advanced capabilities of OmniChem in molecular design and synthesis planning. This work establishes a scalable paradigm utilizing adaptive multi-agents to overcome inherent reasoning deficiencies, offering a feasible pathway for accelerating knowledge discovery in specialized scientific fields.

</details>


### [158] [OmniFood-Bench: Evaluating VLMs for Nutrient Reasoning and Personalized Health Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08423)
*Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Miao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniFood-Bench基准，用于评估大视觉语言模型在食品领域的系统性信息不对称问题。该基准从基础感知、定量推理到安全关键建议三个递进能力维度评估VLM。实验发现模型存在严重的"语义-物理鸿沟"：在菜品命名上接近人类水平，但在质量估计上表现灾难性失败，且常对高风险糖尿病场景生成有害建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要集中在粗粒度的食品分类任务，无法评估真实饮食管理中所需的复杂推理链条——从识别隐藏成分到估算物理质量，再到综合安全性医疗建议。VLMs在食品系统中面临"视觉外观与内在营养构成之间的系统性信息不对称"这一独特且持久的挑战。

Method: 基于MM-Food-100K数据集构建OmniFood-Bench基准，设计三个递进能力评估维度：(1) 基础感知——成分与烹饪方法识别；(2) 定量推理——份量估计与营养分析；(3) 安全关键建议——疾病特异性推荐。评估了包括gpt-5.1、gemini-3-flash、qwen3-vl-8B在内的六种SOTA VLM。

Result: 模型在菜品命名上接近人类水平，但在质量估计上表现灾难性失败，并频繁对高风险糖尿病场景生成看似无害但实际上有害的建议，揭示了显著的"语义-物理鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 该工作为部署于公共卫生领域的自主智能体建立了严格的信任度评估标准。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into critical infrastructure promises to revolutionize
  personalized healthcare and dietary management. However, in the domain of food systems, autonomous agents face a
  unique and persistent challenge: the "Systemic Information Asymmetry" between visual appearance and intrinsic
  nutritional composition. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on coarse-grained classification tasks, such as food
  category recognition, which fail to evaluate the intricate reasoning chain required for real-world dietary management
  -- specifically, the ability to traverse from identifying hidden ingredients to estimating physical mass, and finally
  synthesizing safety-critical medical advice. In this paper, we introduce OmniFood-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark
  constructed from the MM-Food-100K dataset. Unlike previous works, OmniFood-Bench evaluates VLMs across three
  progressive capabilities: Basic Perception (Ingredients & Cooking Methods), Quantitative Reasoning (Portion Size &
  Nutritional Profiling), and Safety-Critical Advisory (Disease-Specific Recommendations). We evaluate six
  state-of-the-art VLMs, including gpt-5.1, gemini-3-flash, and qwen3-vl-8B. Our extensive experiments reveal a
  startling "Semantic-Physical Gap": while models achieve near-human accuracy in naming dishes, they exhibit
  catastrophic failure in mass estimation and frequently hallucinate benign advice for high-risk diabetic profiles. This
  work establishes a rigorous standard for trustworthiness in autonomous agents deployed for public health. The code
  and datasets are available in: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniFood-Bench-7D0B

</details>


### [159] [Applying JEPA-Style Predictive Learning to JA4-Derived Network Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08465)
*Javier Izquierdo,Aygul Zagidullina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 JA4-JEPA，一种基于 JEPA 范式的 Transformer 模型，用于从 JA4 系列网络指纹中学习表征。在 JA4DB 和 CIC-IDS-2017 共约 39.7 万样本上训练，即使不同样本缺乏完整的视图覆盖，模型在 39,416 个保留测试集上仍达到 0.9899 的余弦相似度和 0.9220 的 kNN 准确率，证明 JEPA 式预测学习能有效生成网络指纹嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）这一在图像和视频领域表现优异的自监督学习范式，是否同样适用于紧凑的网络指纹（JA4 系列）表征学习。

Method: 构建 JA4-JEPA，一个基于 Transformer 的模型，在 JA4、JA4H、JA4S 和 JA4X 四个子字段上训练，数据来自 JA4DB 和 CIC-IDS-2017 两个来源（约 39.7 万样本）。模型通过匹配潜在预测与目标编码器输出进行学习，而非重构原始输入。使用冻结的 kNN 探针评估表征质量。

Result: 在 39,416 个保留测试样本上，模型在 TLS、DNS、SSH 的协议族分类任务中达到余弦相似度 0.9899 和 kNN 准确率 0.9220。

Conclusion: JEPA 风格的预测学习能够从 JA4 衍生的网络指纹中生成有用的嵌入表示，即使在数据来源间视图覆盖不完整的情况下依然有效。

Abstract: I-JEPA and V-JEPA learn by matching latent predictions to target encoder outputs rather than regenerating the original input, and this has worked well for images and video. We explore whether the same objective works for compact network fingerprints. We built JA4-JEPA, a Transformer-based model trained on JA4, JA4H, JA4S, and JA4X subfields drawn from JA4DB and CIC-IDS- 2017. The training data combines roughly 397K samples from both sources, though no single sample contains all four view families. We evaluated the learned representations with a frozen kNN probe on protocol-family classification across TLS, DNS, and SSH. On 39,416 heldout samples the model achieved a cosine similarity of 0.9899 and a kNN accuracy of 0.9220. These results indicate that JEPA-style predictive learning can produce useful embeddings from JA4-derived fingerprints, even with incomplete view overlap across sources.
  Keywords: JA4, network fingerprinting, JEPA, predictive representation learning, self-supervised learning

</details>


### [160] [Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) for Adaptive Semantic Modeling in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08490)
*Bharathwaj Vijayakumar,Sahana K. Varadaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种漂移感知时序图重连（DATGR）框架，通过基于语义漂移动态更新共现边权重，在不重训练嵌入的情况下捕获生物医学语言的演化，在BIOMRC数据集上AUROC提升约0.066，AUPRC保持可比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言随新发现快速演化，传统静态嵌入和共现图无法捕获这种语义变化，导致检索和知识发现任务性能下降。

Method: 提出DATGR框架，通过逻辑斯蒂更新规则对边权重进行轻量级、反馈驱动的动态重连，替代逐时间片重训练嵌入的策略，实现基于估计语义漂移的共现边自适应更新。

Result: 在BIOMRC数据集上，AUROC绝对提升约0.066（0.699 vs. 0.633），AUPRC保持可比（0.738 vs. 0.744），表明漂移感知自适应在提升链接预测召回率的同时未损失精确率。

Conclusion: 边缘级别的自适应能有效捕获演化生物医学文本中的时序语义变化，同时保持计算高效性和可解释性。

Abstract: Biomedical language evolves rapidly as new discoveries emerge, causing traditional text models to lose semantic fidelity over time. Static embeddings and co-occurrence graphs cannot capture such evolution, leading to performance degradation in retrieval and knowledge discovery tasks. This paper introduces a Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) framework that models concept evolution by dynamically updating co-occurrence edges based on estimated semantic drift. Instead of retraining embeddings for each time slice, DATGR performs lightweight, feedback-driven rewiring using a logistic update rule applied to edge weights. Evaluated on the Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus (BIOMRC), the method achieved a mean Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) improvement of approximately 0.066 absolute difference (0.699 vs. 0.633) over a static baseline. Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) remained comparable (0.738 vs. 0.744), showing that drift-aware adaptation enhances link-prediction recall without a loss in precision. These results demonstrate that edge-level adaptation effectively captures temporal semantic change in evolving biomedical text while remaining computationally efficient and interpretable.

</details>


### [161] [AI-guided stimuli discovery and generation to optimize facial emotion perception studies in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08533)
*Kushin Mukherjee,Na Yeon Kim,Maren Wehrheim,Ralph Adolphs,Kohitij Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型引导的框架，利用人工神经网络和生成对抗网络来发现和转化能揭示自闭症与神经典型成人之间感知差异的面部表情刺激，证明了群体差异集中在少数诊断性表情上，而非均匀分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自闭症与神经典型成人在面部情绪感知上的群体差异在不同研究中结果不一，作者假设这种变异性源于图像级别的稀疏性——差异集中在少数诊断性表情上，因此需要一种更灵敏、可靠且机制信息丰富的行为测定方法。

Method: 1) 训练群体特异性人工神经网络模型预测图像级别的情绪判断；2) 用模型选择能最大化群体分离的新面孔；3) 在独立队列中验证模型所选图像比随机图像产生更大的行为差异；4) 使用生成对抗网络将诊断性图像转化为预测的群体一致图像，并在表型匹配验证中测试。

Result: 模型选择的图像在独立队列中比随机匹配图像产生更大的行为差异；合成图像相比原始图像减少了行为分离度。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了行为表型分析可以从固定刺激集的平均化转向优化测定，识别神经多样性感知在何种条件下存在差异或趋同，为研究群体感知差异提供了新范式。

Abstract: Understanding perceptual differences between autistic and neurotypical adults requires behavioral assays that are sensitive, reliable, and mechanistically informative. Facial emotion perception is a useful test case because group differences have been reported, but findings vary across studies. Here we show that this variability may reflect image-level sparsity: autistic-neurotypical differences in emotion judgments were concentrated in a small subset of diagnostic facial expressions rather than spread uniformly across stimuli. We trained population-specific artificial neural network models to predict image-level judgments for autistic and neurotypical participants, then used these models to select novel faces predicted to maximize group separation. In an independent cohort, model-selected images produced larger behavioral differences than matched random images. We then used the same models with a generative adversarial network to transform diagnostic images toward greater predicted group agreement. In phenotype-matched validation, synthesized images reduced behavioral separation relative to their matched originals. These results establish a model-guided framework for discovering and transforming stimuli that reveal population-specific perceptual differences. More broadly, they show how behavioral phenotyping can move beyond averaging across fixed stimulus sets toward optimized assays that identify the conditions under which neurodivergent perception diverges or converges.

</details>


### [162] [CommuniWave:A Machine Learning Model for Quantifying the Degree of Temporary Informal Behavior in Urban Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08554)
*Hongye Yang,Shien Liu,Zhihao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CommuniWave机器学习模型，通过整合行为捕捉网络（BCN）、自研YOLOv10模型（YLX）和随机森林行为评估模型（BEM），从街道视频中量化检测城市社区的非正规行为程度（DIB），生成波动图支持动态监测，帮助城市管理者做出精细决策以提升社区韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市社区面临复杂性和不确定性，现有规划采用自上而下方式，缺乏量化居民非正规行为的有效指标，导致规划与实际行为频繁冲突，亟需一种动态监测方法以增强社区韧性。

Method: 基于mmaction2的行为捕捉网络（BCN）+ 自研YOLOv10模型（YLX）用于目标检测 + 随机森林行为评估模型（BEM），三者集成生成非正规行为程度（DIB）波动图。

Result: 能够从街道视频中生成DIB波动图，实现城市社区非正规行为的动态监测与量化评估。

Conclusion: CommuniWave模型可支持城市管理者进行精细化决策，从而提升社区整体韧性。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [163] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF）方法，一种基于树结构的单模态与跨模态专家混合模型。重点研究了当专家具有不等特征维度时，SHAP归因缩减方式（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs）的影响。实验表明，sum-abs缩减几乎匹配甚至略超早期融合效果，显著优于后期融合。主要贡献来自跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂的逐样本路由机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期融合准确但单一整体化，后期融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互。本文旨在探索一种兼顾模块化与跨模态交互的融合方法，并深入研究SHAP归因缩减方式对不等维度专家的影响，提供透明的实证分析。

Method: 提出XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF），采用树结构混合单模态与跨模态专家，通过TreeSHAP归因幅度计算样本级权重。对比三种SHAP缩减方式（mean-abs、median-abs、sum-abs），在MELD（7类情感识别）和CMU-MOSEI（3类情感识别）数据集上，结合三种人脸序列聚合器（含Transformer变体）进行实验。

Result: MELD上sum-abs XGAF（Transformer变体）达0.5983 wF，接近早期融合0.6018，显著优于后期融合0.4598（McNemar检验p<0.0001）。CMU-MOSEI上sum-abs XGAF达0.6519 wF，略超早期融合0.6485和后期融合0.5696。消融实验表明主要增益来自跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂路由。mean-abs和median-abs权重接近均匀分布，sum-abs权重集中于三模态专家。

Conclusion: sum-abs缩减能有效保留总归因质量，避免高维跨模态专家被抑制，实现接近甚至略优于早期融合的性能，同时保持模块化优势。论文的透明实证分析揭示了SHAP缩减方式、专家维度与跨模态专家设计对模块化多模态融合的影响。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [164] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCC-STAR是一个基于大语言模型的临床决策支持系统，它通过读取电子病历叙事文本，同时输出风险分层分期、排序后的指南一致治疗建议及个体化生存预测。在来自中国12家医院的6,668名患者的多中心队列中，HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层方面达到了最先进水平，患者中位生存期从29-32个月提升至51个月，性能超越临床指南、GPT-5和Gemini-2.5 Pro。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肝细胞癌（HCC）分期系统和指南只能提供粗略分类，忽略了同一分期内的异质性以及电子病历（EMR）中的临床上下文信息，导致无法实现精准的个体化治疗决策。

Method: 1）从SEER数据库收集约30,000例HCC病例，通过临床医生验证的提示增强工作流生成为EMR风格的叙事训练数据；2）构建知识对齐的推理框架，采用逐步可验证的复合奖励进行优化；3）模型同时输出基于风险评分的分期、排序后的指南一致治疗方案（附循证依据）以及个体化生存预测。

Result: 1）在12家医院6,668名患者的多中心队列中达到SOTA性能，优于BCLC/CNLC指南、GPT-5和Gemini-2.5 Pro；2）遵循HCC-STAR推荐的患者中位生存期为51个月，而BCLC和CNLC分别为29和32个月；3）盲评中专家认为其推理和循证理由可信；4）模型在治疗准确性上超越住院医师和主治医师，辅助使用时能帮助医生更快做出更准确的决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR是一种可靠且可验证的临床决策支持系统，能够有效改善HCC的风险分层和精准治疗决策。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [165] [The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08625)
*João Matos,Olivia Buege,Donny Cheung,Gary S. Collins,Paula Dhiman,Nan Li,Bingyu Mao,Benjamin W. Nelson,Michail Ouroutzoglou,Paul Varghese,Jonathan Amar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 真实患者与健康聊天机器人的对话模式多样，基于理想化模拟患者开发的系统在实际应用中表现不佳。本文通过分析2053条真实对话，构建了模拟临床内容、情感状态、会话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器，发现沟通风格显著影响分诊结果，凸显了AI系统需适应真实世界沟通多样性的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向消费者的健康聊天机器人（基于大语言模型）在开发与评估中往往依赖于合作、表达清晰、理想化的模拟患者，忽略了真实用户沟通模式的多样性。这种训练与评估方式可能导致系统在真实部署时性能下降并放大健康差距。

Method: 分析了2,053条真实患者-聊天机器人对话；构建了一个患者模拟器，分别建模临床内容、情感状态、会话策略和沟通风格四个维度；使用15名人类评分者进行图灵测试式真实性评估；使用五种不同的患者人物角色，在1,164个由临床医生评分的案例中评估了四个大语言模型的紧急程度评估能力。

Result: 在图灵测试中，模拟对话与真实对话几乎无法区分，人类评分者的准确率仅为55%。沟通风格显著改变了分诊结果。

Conclusion: 以患者为中心的健康会话AI必须容纳沟通多样性；针对理想化而非真实交互设计的系统在现实世界中存在性能不足和加剧健康差距的风险。

Abstract: Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.

</details>


### [166] [Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08652)
*Eugene Ng Yi Sheng,Bingquan Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在无约束的重复社会困境中，自私智能体倾向于背叛，导致合作收益崩溃。本文通过在18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM代理组成的多主体市场模拟中，测试多种形式化机制（尤其中介机制）在对抗性攻击下的稳定性，发现中介机制能抵抗最优攻击，虽受损害但市场不崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自私主体在无约束的社会困境中会趋向背叛，破坏合作带来的市场收益，因此需要研究何种形式化机制能够维持市场稳定，并考察这些机制在面对对抗性攻击时的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建多智能体市场模拟：18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM代理具备互补生产专长，在受限社交网络中交易以获取效用。实验分两阶段：（1）在200轮次中对比8种机制条件（渐进注入破坏性角色），识别中介机制为最优；（2）对中介机制进行对抗性红队测试，使用迭代提示优化的LLM驱动破坏者攻击。

Result: 最佳攻击（v6）降低诚实代理效用13.3%，但无法导致市场崩溃。中介机制在持续对抗压力下仍能实现效用恢复。定义对抗鲁棒性为机制在优化攻击下维持诚实代理正效用的能力，中介机制表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 中介机制是维持多智能体市场稳定性的有效形式化机制，具有对抗鲁棒性——可被弯曲但不可被摧毁。

Abstract: Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.

</details>


### [167] [SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08681)
*Shilin Ou,Yifan Xu,Luyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SolarChain-Eval基准，用于评估去中心化能源市场中可信赖的经济智能体。该基准将市场治理建模为MDP，从市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性等多个维度评估策略，并引入基于LLM的规划器/审计器层。实验揭示了效用-安全权衡，RL智能体虽提升市场效用但可能产生不安全行为，LLM审计器可缓解部分风险但无法完全替代正确的奖励函数设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主AI系统在物理-网络环境中的应用日益广泛，需要同时评估任务性能和可信赖性。在去中心化能源市场中，自主智能体可能利用无效物理数据、制造虚假流动性、产生不稳定的治理决策，因此需要一种物理约束的评估基准。

Method: 将市场治理构建为Gymnasium兼容的MDP，智能体每小时做出决策；从六个维度评估策略；引入LLM Planner/Auditor层，Planner定义episode级动作边界和审计规则，Auditor审查并修订高风险动作；所有干预通过结构化日志记录。

Result: 静态、随机、贪心、RL和RL+LLM策略的实验揭示了效用-安全权衡。RL智能体提升市场效用但可产生不安全行为；移除物理惩罚后，奖励最大化智能体会利用无效发电并增加虚假流动性；LLM Planner/Auditor提升可审计性并缓解部分风险，但无法完全补偿错误指定的奖励函数。

Conclusion: 可信赖的智能体AI评估需要物理约束和透明的干预轨迹。奖励函数设计至关重要，LLM审计器不能替代正确设计的奖励函数。

Abstract: As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.

</details>


### [168] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"行为状态衰减"问题，即长程任务中决策相关信息随轨迹增长被埋没。通过引入独立的记忆智能体，主动维护结构化记忆库并选择性注入记忆提醒，在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上分别提升+8.3pp和+6.8pp pass@1，消融实验验证了选择性干预优于被动检索等基线，并探索了基于SETA数据训练的开源记忆策略模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程任务中，决策相关信息分散在越来越长的轨迹中，现有方法（被动检索、全量注入等）无法有效让动作智能体在关键时刻获取所需记忆，导致"行为状态衰减"（behavioral state decay）。

Method: 提出一个独立的记忆智能体（memory agent），与未修改的动作智能体并行运行。记忆智能体从近期轨迹更新结构化记忆库，并自主决定是否向动作智能体注入记忆驱动的提醒（memory-grounded reminder）或保持静默。该模块即插即用，兼容前沿动作智能体和现有智能体框架。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0上pass@1提升+8.3个百分点，在τ²-Bench上提升+6.8个百分点。消融实验显示选择性干预优于被动记忆库暴露、始终注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索。基于SETA数据训练Qwen3.5-27B（SFT+GRPO）提升了验证奖励，并实现了向Terminal-Bench的部分迁移。

Conclusion: 记忆作为主动干预机制而非被动检索，能显著缓解长程任务中的行为状态衰减问题；选择性干预是最优策略；基于强化学习（GRPO）训练开源记忆策略模型是可行的初步方向。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [169] [The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08734)
*Baha Rababah,Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora,Carson K. Leung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 量化模型在准确率和困惑度指标上看似表现良好，但实际决策行为与原始模型存在显著差异；提出正确性一致性指标（correctness agreement）来量化这种行为偏差，并揭示注意力层中查询和关键投影对量化更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量化评估仅依赖准确率和困惑度，无法捕捉量化引起的模型行为变化，导致"等价幻觉"——即看似性能不变但决策行为已发生偏移。

Method: 提出正确性一致性（correctness agreement）这一决策级指标，用于测量基模型与量化变体之间正确预测的重叠程度；将量化操作视为注意力权重的结构算子，使用统计和分布度量逐层分析量化失真。

Result: 在中等量化程度下，即使任务性能看似保持不变，行为偏差已经出现；低比特宽度下存在非线性突变点；查询和关键投影（query/key projections）比值和输出投影（value/output projections）对量化更敏感。

Conclusion: 基模型与量化模型之间存在"等价幻觉"，需要超越传统性能指标的行为评估方法。

Abstract: Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.

</details>


### [170] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受Lisp启发的、语言无关的概念模型，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系统一表示为持久化知识对象，核心语义区分在于确定性计算（derive）与受控LLM推理（infer），从而实现工作流本身作为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用使用显式工作流处理工具调用、检索、分支、检查点和人工审批等执行问题，但缺乏统一的语义持久化概念框架，难以将工作流自身表示为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。

Method: 采用符号形式、对象恒等性和活镜像思维作为解释性透镜，提出derive（确定性计算）与infer（受控LLM判断）的核心语义区分，所有工作流相关对象均表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。

Result: 形成了初步的语义持久化概念框架：工作流不仅是产生知识并留下痕迹的执行过程，其本身也可以被表示为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。形式化转移语义学留作未来工作。

Conclusion: 该概念模型为LLM工作流的持久化表示提供了一种新的视角，但尚缺乏完整的形式化语义支持。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [171] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AUTOPILOT-VQA，一个以事故为中心的驾驶视频问答基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在安全关键场景中的时序推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景理解、决策等任务上有所进展，但缺乏评估其对安全关键事件可靠推理能力的标准化基准。

Method: 构建事故中心的行车记录仪视频问答数据集，设计涵盖天气光照、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标志标线、涉及实体、事故发生、撞击位置及可避免性推理等多类安全相关类别的结构化问题。

Result: 形成标准化基准，支持对自动驾驶系统在多样化场景下可靠性的评估，推动可解释、鲁棒、安全导向的视觉语言系统发展。

Conclusion: AUTOPILOT-VQA超越传统目标识别，实现面向时序接地和安全感知的推理，并作为CVPR 2026竞赛基准发布。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [172] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench)，一个评估AI系统科学谱系推理和谱系驱动想法生成能力的基准。包含1,961条谱系轨迹、1,085个Idea基因组对象和920个GenomeDiff记录，覆盖10个科学领域。通过IG-Exam（42种任务类型）和IG-Arena（基于种群进化评分PES）两项评估，实验表明最强系统在谱系推理上仅达27.3%的精确准确率，且结构化谱系上下文会打乱系统排名而非均匀提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少评估AI系统能否像科学思想进化那样，通过继承机制、修复已知局限、重组早期工作来产生新想法。科学思想的产生并非从零开始，而是具有类似生物基因组的继承结构，但当前缺乏对此类能力的系统化评估框架。

Method: 基于IdeaGene框架，将论文或提案表示为最小化、带类型、有证据支撑的Idea基因组对象集合，通过GenomeDiff记录继承、突变、丢失、外部引入和新插入六种演化动力学。构建含1,961条黄金谱系轨迹、1,085个Idea基因组对象和920个GenomeDiff记录的数据集，覆盖10个科学领域。设计IG-Exam（42种任务类型，1,029个实例）评估封闭式谱系推理，IG-Arena评估谱系条件化生成（使用种群进化评分PES）。

Result: 在14个基于LLM的科学系统上进行实验，最强系统在谱系推理上仅达到27.3%的精确准确率。结构化谱系上下文会重新排序系统排名，而非对所有参与者产生均匀提升效果。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在科学谱系推理和谱系驱动的想法生成方面存在显著的组合瓶颈，谱系结构化信息对系统的帮助并非普遍有效，揭示了现有模型在理解科学思想进化结构方面的根本局限。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [fog: Expressing Motion and Emotion through Function Composition of AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07952)
*Vivian Liu,Lydia Chilton*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了fog，一个函数组合框架，用于实现和组合运动函数，可表达Heider-Simmel风格动画中的运动与情感。通过代码生成和动画编辑器，用户可创建开放的运动词汇（动词、副词、手势、情感）。感知评估显示其运动识别准确率达68%（比随机基线高2.68倍），用户研究证明其能支持快速迭代、探索和控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动与情感是智能、可表达行为的核心部分，现有工具在生成和组合富有语义的运动函数方面存在不足，缺乏既灵活又易于控制的框架来创建开放式的运动词汇。

Method: 提出fog函数组合框架，支持将运动函数进行组合；配套动画编辑器支持直接操纵和动态生成UI；通过代码生成帮助用户创建动词、副词、手势和情感等运动函数。

Result: 452个fog生成的动画中，运动语义识别准确率为68%，相比随机基线提升2.68倍；混合方法用户研究表明fog界面支持更快的迭代、探索和控制。

Conclusion: fog框架能有效表达运动与情感语义，在可识别性和用户效率方面均优于基线，为开放式运动词汇的创作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motion and emotion are core parts of intelligent, expressive behavior. In this paper, we introduce fog, a function composition framework for implementing and compose motion functions. We demonstrate how fog can be used to express motion and emotion in Heider-Simmel style animations. This code generation framework can help users generate functions for verbs, adverbs, gestures, and emotions to create an open-ended motion vocabulary. It is complemented by an animation editor that helps users refine motion through direct manipulation and dynamically generated UI. We evaluate our approach with a perceptual evaluation, where we test 452 fog-generated animations to see if people can recognize the semantic meaning of the motion. We find that fog's motion functions can be recognized at 68% accuracy, a 2.68x improvement over a chance baseline. In a mixed-methods user study with professionals and novices, we show that fog in interface form can support users with more rapid iteration, exploration, and control.

</details>


### [174] [The Behavioural Reflection Test: A time-efficient measure of reflective reasoning in morally and epistemically charged decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07961)
*Sion Weatherhead,Flora Salim,Aaron Belbasis,Ben R. Newell*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出行为反思测试（BRT）和定制版认知反思测试（bCRT），以解决传统CRT因题目曝光而失效的问题。研究发现，bCRT能预测更敏感于证据、伦理驱动的决策和对高质量信息的依赖，以及更具情感投入、风险关注、经济性的语言风格，而传统CRT无法恢复这些关联。BRT作为开放式测量，具有跨领域扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统认知反思测试（CRT）经典题目因广泛曝光而失效，且无法反映决策风格和语言表达中的反思倾向。亟需一种低曝光、行为化的测量工具，以评估人们在道德和认知情境中的反思能力。

Method: 提出行为反思测试（BRT）作为开放式测量，配合四题定制版认知反思测试（bCRT）作为低曝光锚定。在473名在线成年人中施测，检验bCRT与决策质量、信息源选择及语言特征的关系，并与传统CRT进行对比。

Result: bCRT得分越高，越倾向于证据敏感、伦理驱动的决策，依赖高质量信息源，语言风格更具情感投入、风险关注和经济性。传统CRT无法恢复这些关联。bCRT展现出聚合效度，额外提供项目信息。BRT中位完成时间11.8分钟，时间效率高。

Conclusion: bCRT是传统CRT的有效替代方案，能捕捉到传统CRT无法反映的反思倾向在决策风格和语言中的表现。BRT作为开放式行为测量，具备跨领域扩展的潜力。

Abstract: How readily people override intuitive conclusions through reflection shapes how they navigate dense information environments with reliable and misleading sources; yet the effectiveness of a prominent measure, the Cognitive Reflection Test (CRT), is eroded by widespread exposure to classic items and leaves open how such tendencies manifest more generally in decision style and linguistic expression. The Behavioural Reflection Test (BRT) addresses these issues with a brief open-ended measure of reasoning in morally and epistemically charged scenarios, alongside a four-item bespoke CRT (bCRT) as a low-exposure anchor. Among 473 online adults, higher bCRT predicted more evidence-sensitive, ethically driven decisions and reliance on high-quality sources, marked by more emotionally engaged, risk-attentive, economical language; associations the familiarity-adjusted CRT did not recover. The bCRT showed convergent validity, added item information above mean ability. Though open-ended, the BRT remained a time-efficient (median 11.8 minutes) behavioural assay of reflection with scope to extend across domains.

</details>


### [175] [HeadRoom: Lightweight, Edge-deployable Pipeline for Adaptive Notification Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08083)
*Dinithi Dissanayake,Prasanth Sasikumar,Suranga Nanayakkara*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出HeadRoom，一种可从自我中心视频和音频实时估计视觉与听觉通道可用性的轻量级边缘可部署管道，并通过用户研究验证了在高感知负载下将通知路由至更可用通道能缩短响应时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）可通过多感官通道传递通知，但目前缺乏如何在合适时机选择合适通道的理解。

Method: 提出HeadRoom——一种轻量级、边缘可部署的管道，利用自我中心视频和音频实时估计视觉和听觉通道的可用性，并通过受控用户研究（N=25）进行验证。

Result: 在高感知负载下，将通知路由至更可用通道相比路由至较不可用通道能显著缩短响应时间。

Conclusion: 该工作为可穿戴和沉浸式系统中的通知自适应路由开辟了新可能性。

Abstract: Emerging wearables, such as smart glasses, can deliver notifications through multiple sensory channels, but there is still a limited understanding of how to choose the right channel at the right moment. We propose HeadRoom, a lightweight, edge-deployable pipeline that estimates the availability of visual and auditory channels in real time from egocentric video and audio. Our controlled user study (N=25) shows that, under high perceptual load, routing notifications to the more available channel reduces response time relative to routing them to the less available channel. This work opens up a new possibility for adaptive routing of notifications in wearable and immersive systems.

</details>


### [176] [How Analysts Use AI in High-Stakes Crime Linkage: An Industrial Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08274)
*Jessica Woodhams,Amy Burrell,Wanyin Li,Fahim Ahmed,Matthew Tonkin,Jan Lemeire,Arkady Konovalov,Steven Frisson,Mark Webb,Sarah Galambos,Vesna Nowack,Dalal Alrajeh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文报告了一款与英国执法机构联合开发的人工智能犯罪关联分析辅助工具的工业评估。通过结合直接观察、眼动追踪、鼠标追踪和调查的混合方法可用性研究，发现分析师有选择性地使用AI预测，常以行为证据进行验证，反映出部分信任和对传统分析方法的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 犯罪关联分析在实际工作中费时费力且认知负担大，分析师需手动在海量犯罪数据库中寻找行为与情境联系，且可能反复接触令人不适的内容。因此需要开发AI辅助工具来支持这一工作。

Method: 采用混合方法的可用性研究，结合直接观察、眼动追踪、鼠标追踪和问卷调查，考察分析师如何与AI预测及作为解释呈现的模型特征进行交互。

Result: 分析师有选择性地使用AI预测，并经常以行为（非AI）证据进行验证，反映部分信任和对既有分析实践的持续依赖；分析师关注呈现的模型特征并认可其可用性，同时指出了解释呈现和工作流整合方面的改进空间。

Conclusion: AI辅助决策工具需要更好地整合解释性内容与传统分析方法，并凸显了在高压高风险场景中进行现场评估对于工程化可用且可信的AI系统的重要性。

Abstract: Crime linkage analysis is used in many countries to identify series of offences that may have been committed by the same individual. In practice, specialist analysts manually search for behavioural and situational connections across large crime databases, an effort that is time-consuming, cognitively demanding, and can involve repeated exposure to disturbing material. To support this work, an Artificial Intelligence (AI)-enabled decision-support tool was co-developed with a UK law enforcement agency to assist analysts in identifying likely crime linkages.
  This paper reports an industrial evaluation of the crime-linkage tool. We conducted a mixed-methods usability study combining direct observation, eye-tracking, mouse-tracking, and surveys to examine how analysts engage with AI predictions and with the model features presented as explanations. Our findings show that analysts used the AI predictions selectively and frequently validated them against behavioural (non-AI) evidence, reflecting partial trust and an ongoing reliance on established analytical practices. We also found that analysts attended to the presented model features and valued their availability, while identifying opportunities to improve how explanations are presented and integrated into the workflow. Overall, our results highlight the need for AI-enabled decision-support tools to better integrate explanations and traditional analytical methods, and demonstrate the importance of in-situ evaluation for engineering usable and trustworthy AI in high-stakes settings.

</details>


### [177] [ImputeViz: A Visual Analytics Dashboard for Diagnosing Missing Data and Comparing Imputation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08579)
*Aitik Dandapat,Lalith Punepalle Raveendrareddy,Mithilesh Kumar Singh,Klaus Mueller*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ImputeViz是一个集成可视化分析仪表板，支持缺失数据诊断、插补模型配置与结果评估，引入地理感知kNN（gKNN）方法，并通过协调视图实现跨方法比较，帮助分析者识别缺失模式、选择有效策略并评估模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺失数据是科学和社会科学研究中的持续障碍，常导致分析偏倚，且分析者承担处理缺失值的责任，现有工具缺乏对缺失模式诊断、多方法比较及地理空间推理的集成支持。

Method: 集成MICE、Random Forest、XGBoost、kNN等常用方法，提出地理感知kNN（gKNN）变体；构建协调视图（热图、共缺失摘要、分布诊断）支持MCAR/MAR/MNAR模式推理；实现方法比较摘要（MAE、RMSE、Delta RMSE、运行时间）与变量级差异视图；采用缓存结果与锁定轴尺度降低认知开销。

Result: 案例研究证明ImputeViz能帮助分析者选择有效插补策略、识别敏感变量并评估模型鲁棒性；跨方法比较可揭示方法分歧点、变量敏感性与插补选择对下游摘要的影响。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是一个方法无关的可视分析环境，将跨方法比较作为首要可视化任务，集成gKNN与标准方法，提供基于来源的可视化问责机制，支持分析者做出更明智的缺失数据处理决策。

Abstract: Missing data is a persistent obstacle in scientific, social science, and public health research, often biasing analyses and placing accountability on analysts for how they handle missing values. We introduce ImputeViz, an integrated visual analytics dashboard that supports diagnosing missingness, configuring imputation models, and evaluating results. The system brings together widely used methods, including MICE, Random Forest, XGBoost, and kNN, within an interactive environment that makes missingness patterns explicit. To support geospatial reasoning, we introduce gKNN, a geographically informed kNN variant that blends socioeconomic and spatial distances and exposes donor contributions, enabling provenance-based visual accountability by showing which regions drive each estimate. Our primary contribution is a method-agnostic visual analytics environment that makes cross-method comparison a first-class visual task and integrates gKNN alongside standard methods. Coordinated views reveal missingness structure through heatmaps, co-missingness summaries, and distributional diagnostics that help analysts reason about missingness patterns (MCAR/MAR) and cases where missingness may be non-random (MNAR). Users can compare and tune models and interrogate results via distributional overlays, a Method Comparison Summary reporting MAE, RMSE, Delta RMSE, and runtime for each algorithm on the current target and mask, along with variable-level discrepancy views. Cached per-method results and locked axis scales reduce cognitive overhead from shifting ranges during method switching. These comparisons highlight where methods disagree, which variables are sensitive, and how imputation choices affect downstream summaries. Case studies demonstrate how ImputeViz helps analysts select effective strategies, surface sensitive variables, and assess model robustness.

</details>


### [178] [How YouTube Frames ChatGPT Use in Education: An Epistemic Network Analysis with Supporting Multimodal Metadata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08698)
*Shayla Sharmin,Mohammad Al-Ratrout,Mohammad Fahim Abrar,Roghayeh Leila Barmaki*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过多模态元数据（转录、标题、缩略图、评论）分析了52个教育类YouTube视频，发现三类话语群体：将ChatGPT作为概念支架（G1）、检索练习与技能构建（G2）、输出生成工具（G3）。G3虽达到与G2相当的传播量，但学习导向显著弱化，揭示了自导式AI学习中"高传播、低深度"的结构性张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（如ChatGPT）在非正式、创作者驱动的公共话语中如何被呈现给学习者，目前尚不清楚。现有研究缺乏对教育类YouTube视频中LLM框架的实证分析，特别是在不同创作者群体间这些框架如何差异以及如何影响受众反馈和平台传播。

Method: 遵循PRISMA指南筛选52个视频，利用多模态元数据（转录文本、标题、缩略图和观众评论）进行分析。采用认知网络分析（Epistemic Network Analysis）识别话语群体差异，并进行组间统计检验。

Result: 识别出三个结构不同的话语群体：G1（概念支架）、G2（检索练习与技能构建）、G3（输出生成工具）。认知网络分析显示显著的组间差异且效应量较大。多模态元数据（转录、标题、缩略图）一致反映了这些区别。G3的视频达到与G2相当的传播量，但学习导向框架显著更弱。

Conclusion: 内容优先快速输出的视频比促进深度参与的视频传播更广，揭示了自导式AI学习中"高传播、低深度"的结构性张力。这一差距引发了对何种AI素养能够被规模化传播以及学习者实际收获的批判性反思。

Abstract: We examine educational YouTube videos through multimodal metadata, such as transcripts, titles, thumbnails, and viewer comments, to investigate how ChatGPT is framed across creator groups and how those framings relate to audience response and platform reach. Little is known about how large language models are presented to learners in informal, creator-driven public discourse. Following PRISMA, we selected 52 videos for analysis. We identified three structurally distinct discourse groups: (G1) videos that positioned ChatGPT as a conceptual scaffold for thinking, (G2) videos oriented toward retrieval practice and skill-building, and (G3) videos that framed ChatGPT as a tool for output generation. Epistemic Network Analysis revealed statistically significant group differences with large effect sizes. Multimodal metadata consistently reflected these distinctions across transcript discourse, titles, and thumbnails. Viewers of learning-oriented content described ChatGPT as a thinking partner or tutor, whereas viewers of output-oriented content raised concerns about over-reliance, surface-level learning, and cognitive offloading. G3 achieved comparable platform reach to G2, yet with substantially weaker learning-oriented framing. This may suggest that output-oriented content competes for visibility despite lower pedagogical depth. These findings reveal a structural tension in self-directed AI learning: content that prioritizes quick outputs reaches far more learners than content that promotes deep engagement. This gap raises critical questions about whose vision of AI literacy scales and what learners are actually left with.

</details>


### [179] [Sculptable Mesh Structures for Room-Scale Form-Finding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08736)
*Jesse T. Gonzalez,Yanzhen Zhang,Dian Zhu,Alice Yu,Sapna Tayal,Nazm Furniturewala,Ziying Qi,Somin Ella Moon,Leyi Han,Alexandra Ion,Scott E. Hudson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用户可调节、房间规模的"形状感知"网格结构，用于低 fidelity 原型制作。用户通过物理拉伸和缩短网格边缘来塑造粗糙形态，边缘配备电阻式长度传感器，可将配置传输至计算机，并能在软件中复现，连接原型制作与计算设计流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在计算机显示器内设计物理结构很困难，实际中设计师需要经过一系列低 fidelity 原型（纸、粘土、泡沫）制作过程。这一过程耗时且生成的几何形状难以转化为 CAD 环境进行进一步精炼。

Method: 提出一种房间规模的"形状感知"网格结构，用户通过物理方式拉伸和缩短网格边缘来改变整体曲率并雕刻粗糙形态。边缘配备电阻式长度传感器，可将配置信息传输至中央计算机，并能在软件中复现。

Result: 该结构实现了物理原型到软件的连接，使得低 fidelity 原型阶段能够与更大的计算设计流程衔接。

Conclusion: 该形状感知网格结构能够弥合物理原型与数字设计之间的鸿沟，提高从低 fidelity 原型到 CAD 精炼的过渡效率。

Abstract: It can be hard to design a physical structure entirely within the confines of a computer monitor. To better capture the interplay between real-world objects and a designer's work-in-progress, practitioners will often go through a sequence of low-fidelity prototypes (paper, clay, foam) before arriving at a form that satisfies both functional and aesthetic concerns. While necessary, this model-making process can be quite time-consuming, particularly at larger scales, and the resulting geometry can be difficult to translate into a CAD environment, where it will be further refined.
  This paper introduces a user-adjustable, room-scale, "shape-aware" mesh structure for low-fidelity prototyping. A user physically manipulates the mesh by lengthening and shortening the edges, altering the overall curvature and sculpting coarse forms. The edges are equipped with resistive length sensors, and transmit their configuration to a central computer. The structure can later be reproduced in software, connecting this prototyping stage to the larger computational design pipeline.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [180] [SASGeo: Stability-Aware Semantic Map Localization for GNSS-Denied UAVs -- A Framework and Synthetic Proof of Concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07737)
*Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SAS，一个基于语义地图的定位框架，通过道路、建筑等持久性结构实现GNSS拒止环境下的无人机绝对定位。在220次随机检索试验中，空间语义匹配变体达到94.5-95.5%的Recall@1，优于全局语义描述子的58.6%，但各变体间差异不显著。该实验作为可控扰动下的概念验证，尚未验证实际飞行导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNSS拒止环境下无人机依赖视觉惯性里程计存在漂移，需要绝对位置修正。跨视角图像检索易受季节、光照、视角、地图时效和传感器模态影响，因此需要一种对环境变化鲁棒的语义定位方法。

Method: 提出SAS框架，包含：(1)语义栅格对齐；(2)关系图证据；(3)特征稳定性与地理区分度分析；(4)显式的正/矛盾/未知观测分类；(5)完整性感知的模糊定位拒绝机制。论文明确了具体的权重与决策模型，并通过合成数据进行可重复的概念验证实验。

Result: 在220次随机检索试验中（包含旋转、尺度变化、部分裁剪、遮挡、模拟地图变化和硬语义干扰），全局语义描述子Recall@1为58.6%，空间语义匹配变体达94.5-95.5%。Wilson 95%置信区间显示全局描述子与空间变体间差异显著，但各空间变体间区间重叠，未证明各模块的独立贡献。

Conclusion: 结构化语义几何在受控跨视角扰动下能够有效区分位置，但需要进一步解决别名混淆、地图时效性问题和定位拒绝测试，且尚未在实际飞行导航中验证。

Abstract: GNSS-denied unmanned aerial vehicles require occasional absolute position fixes to bound the drift of visual-inertial odometry. Cross-view image retrieval can provide such fixes, but raw appearance is sensitive to season, illumination, viewpoint, map age, and sensor modality. We propose \sas, a semantic map-localization framework that represents the environment through persistent structures such as roads, buildings, waterways, railways, intersections, and field boundaries. The method combines semantic raster alignment, relational graph evidence, feature stability and geographic distinctiveness, explicit positive/contradictory/unknown observations, and integrity-aware rejection of ambiguous fixes. Unlike a broad architecture-only proposal, this paper specifies concrete weighting and decision models and reports a reproducible synthetic proof of concept. In 220 randomized retrieval trials with rotation, scale changes, partial crops, occlusion, simulated map changes, and hard semantic decoys, a global semantic descriptor achieved 58.6\% Recall@1, while spatial semantic matching variants achieved 94.5-95.5%. Wilson 95\% intervals separate the global descriptor from the spatial variants but overlap among the spatial variants, so the experiment supports semantic geometry rather than a definitive benefit from each proposed module. The preliminary experiment does not validate real-flight navigation; rather, it demonstrates that structured semantic geometry can discriminate locations under controlled cross-view perturbations and identifies the harder aliasing, map-aging, and rejection tests required next.

</details>


### [181] [Physics-Guided Biomechanical Gait Adaptation for Humanoid Locomotion on Extreme Sloped Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07830)
*Xuanyu Chen,Mohan Liu,Dengchen Mei,Zhihao Gu,Haitian Zhang,Kaimin Mao,Haiyue Zhu,Shijun Yan,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段物理引导框架HumoSlope，通过斜率自适应ZMP正则化（Stage I）和生物力学斜坡步态适配器BSGA（Stage II），实现盲人形机器人在高达62.7%（32.1°）户外草坡上的鲁棒行走，避免保守低重心蹲伏步态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无模型强化学习在陡坡控制上尚未充分探索，斜坡地形带来的持续重力偏差要求同时控制稳定性和姿态，而在通用奖励设计下策略容易收敛到缓慢保守的低重心蹲伏步态。

Method: 两阶段框架HumoSlope：Stage I引入斜率自适应ZMP正则化，在局部倾斜支撑平面而非世界水平参考面上评估ZMP；Stage II提出生物力学斜坡步态适配器BSGA，利用提取的宏观地形描述子作为特权训练信号，动态门控软奖励先验，根据估计的坡度几何调节CoM高度和下肢协调（上坡髋主导、下坡膝主导）。部署的actor仅需本体感知。

Result: 在Sim-to-Real实验中，框架有效缓解姿态退化，实现盲人机器人连续穿越高达62.7%（32.1°）的户外草坡。

Conclusion: 验证了物理引导方法在挑战性斜坡地形自适应中的有效性，无需在线外部感知即可实现鲁棒斜坡行走。

Abstract: Model-free reinforcement learning has enabled impressive humanoid locomotion; however, control on steep slopes remains largely unexplored. Unlike flat or discrete terrains, sloped terrains impose a persistent gravitational bias that demands simultaneous stability and posture control.
